) AS [t3]

    FROM [T_CitasClientes] AS [t5]
    INNER JOIN [T_GePersonas] AS [t6] ON [t5].[Id_Cliente] = [t6].[Cedula]
) AS [t4] ON [t3].[Id_Cita] = [t4].[Id_Cita]
